# `lsp/` function index

Every top-level `defun` and `defmacro` in [AmosNT_floq/lsp/](AmosNT_floq/lsp/), generated by
[tools/gen_lsp_index.py](tools/gen_lsp_index.py). Do not edit by hand; rerun the script.
For how the pieces fit together see [QUERY_COMPILER.md](QUERY_COMPILER.md).

- **2528** definitions in **139** files; **1307** have a docstring.
- Files appear in **image load order**, i.e. the order their loading starts when following `(load …)` from `init.lsp`: 115 loaded, then 24 that nothing in `lsp/` loads.
- Only loads inside `lsp/` are followed. Some "unloaded" files are loaded from elsewhere (e.g. AQIT, wrappers, experiment scripts) or by hand.
- **FF**: an AmosQL `as foreign '<name>'` declaration in `lsp/` refers to this function.
- **Also in**: the same name is defined elsewhere too. Among loaded files the one loaded *last* wins.
- Definitions inside a top-level `(quote …)` block are disabled and not listed.
- Docstrings are cut to their first line.
